的协作模式，标准不得将 X 或 Y 设为仅由 A 完成，也不得要求回答忽略 B 的贡献。若回答正确描述了 A 与 B 的协作，而标准强制要求‘A 独自完成’，则该标准为无效标准。核验边界：标准中的每个动作描述必须能对应到官方设定的具体执行主体，禁止出现‘假设 A 独自完成了需要 B 协助才能完成的任务’这类逻辑硬伤。", "retrieval_source": "semantic", The mountains in the east provided a challenge for invaders and helped to protect the coastal regions from invasion.\n\nThe Chinese Communists, under the leadership of Mao Zedong, used these geographical realities to their advantage in the fight against the Nationalists and the Japanese. The Communists recognized that they could not defeat the Nationalists and the Japanese by simply attacking their military forces. This model of protracted war was based on the use of propaganda, hit-and-run tactics, and the creation of puppet governments in enemy territory.
